# Device Source Index

This page inventories device-specific documentation known to the project.

It is both an archival audit and a human lookup page. Search by brand, SKU, document title, document number, revision, date, or language.

For multi-product PDFs, the status field records known Device locations using the document's printed pagination and the 1-based PDF page number. Both are kept when they differ.
